## 2026-09-27 15:12 UTC — /abandoned-cart-launch-plan: 30-day always-on Abandoned-Cart launch plan generator (Move #1)

- **What shipped:** New route at `/abandoned-cart-launch-plan` with a one-click "Generate 30-day launch plan" button. Opens a modal with a 30-day day-by-day checklist (Days 1–30 across W1–W4) generated from the operator's saved Abandoned-Cart ROI calculator inputs (`ecom-ops:playbooks:ac-roi:v1`) overlaid with their Your-store AOV/orders/margin (`ecom-ops:your-store:v1`). Snapshot block (verdict + ROI ratio + recovered orders/month + recovered revenue + total send cost + net revenue) computed by `forecastAbandonedCart()` — same math as `scripts/abandoned_cart_roi.py`. Result is paste-ready markdown: copy-to-clipboard OR download `.md` (named `abandoned-cart-launch-plan-YYYY-MM-DD.md`). 30 days, 4 weeks, 30 checkbox-able actions covering: trigger wiring + checkout-started audit, recovery-rate target + ramp schedule, Klaviyo + Postscript flow scaffolds, suppression lists, 3-email build (1h / 24h / 72h) + 2-SMS build (4h / 24h), Triple Whale + Segment attribution, soft-launch 10% → 50% → 100%, D-1 / D-7 / D-14 / D-21 readouts, AOV-band + repeat-buyer cohort slicing, iteration backlog (cadence + subject-line + weakest-step rewrites), final 30-day program readout. CTA button also surfaced on the Abandoned-Cart ROI calculator on `/playbooks` (next to "Reset defaults" and "Copy report") for at-handbook discovery. Sidebar nav entry added (icon ▦).
- **Why it matters:** Closes the gap between "you've tweaked the abandoned-cart calculator" and "the 30-day Klaviyo+Postscript launch is actually in your project tracker" — a top-3 DTC retention-lever flow. Operators typically have the flow scaffolded in Klaviyo but no calendar-paced checklist to drive the launch, ramp, and 4 readouts. The plan reads the operator's REAL checkouts/AOV/recovery-rate/email+SMS unit-cost so a $500k DTC operator who hasn't tweaked the calc gets the plan generated from their real numbers, not the canonical 1,200/$75/10% defaults. Cross-page intelligence: reads from both `ac-roi:v1` and `your-store:v1` so the snapshot is consistent across Overview, Playbooks, and the launch-plan route — same pattern that landed on PDP A/B and Welcome Series.
- **Where to see it:** https://ecommerce-ops-iota.vercel.app/abandoned-cart-launch-plan — or click "📅 30-day launch plan" on the Abandoned-Cart ROI calculator on https://ecommerce-ops-iota.vercel.app/playbooks
- **Feature class:** one-click-action + state-persistence + cross-page-intelligence (reads Abandoned-Cart inputs from /playbooks + Your-store from /)
- **Files added:** `dashboard/app/abandoned-cart-launch-plan/page.tsx` (~155 lines, route + description + calculator embed + 3 supporting cards), `dashboard/src/components/abandoned-cart-launch-plan-button.tsx` (~270 lines, modal + start-date picker + copy/download handlers + Escape-key close + backdrop-click close), `dashboard/src/lib/abandoned-cart-launch-plan.ts` (~390 lines, pure generator: `buildAbandonedCartLaunchPlan()` + `abandonedCartPlanToMarkdown()` + `abandonedCartPlanDayDate()` + 30-day canonical PLAN_DAYS sequence mirroring Klaviyo+Postscript best-practice for 3-email + 2-SMS abandoned-cart cadence)
- **Files modified:** `dashboard/src/components/sidebar.tsx` (sidebar nav entry added), `dashboard/src/components/abandoned-cart-roi.tsx` (new "📅 30-day launch plan" CTA button next to Reset/Copy)
- **Verification:** RED-GREEN — feature was absent before this tick. GREEN — `cd dashboard && NEXT_TELEMETRY_DISABLED=1 npm run build` SUCCEEDED on FIRST attempt; new route emitted at `.next/server/app/abandoned-cart-launch-plan/page.js`; build output confirmed `○ /abandoned-cart-launch-plan  440 B         121 kB`. `vercel deploy --prod --yes` SUCCEEDED → deployment-id `dpl_CpoePLnFRyBZh969bnBwtRJDqHxG`, hostname `dashboard-o9dsg6ijy-mattiasadem-5021s-projects.vercel.app`, READY/target=production. `vercel alias set` rotated canonical alias in **936ms**. ## 2026-09-27 06:00 UTC — /inventory 3PL vs In-house Cost Comparator (interactive-tool + state-persistence + cross-page-intelligence).

- **What shipped:** New interactive calculator mounted on `/inventory` between the page hero and the static 3PL-vs-in-house / Major-3PLs / Forecasting / CCC cards. The operator enters the in-house cost stack (FTE headcount × loaded $/hr × warehouse lease × inbound freight × misc ops × returns processing time) AND a 3PL quote (pick-pack per order × storage per pallet × receiving per hour) — the panel computes both monthly cost stacks side-by-side, the annualized delta, the break-even monthly order volume above which in-house wins, the ship-time savings (0.5–3.0 days P50), the per-order delta, and assigns a 5-bucket verdict tag (STRONG 3PL / LEAN 3PL / PARITY / LEAN IN-HOUSE / STRONG IN-HOUSE) with a 1-line operator-handoff justification. Defaults match research/05 §3PL-vs-in-house canonical bench (2,500 orders/mo × $75 AOV × 3 FTE × $28/hr loaded × $8,500/mo lease × 12 pallets × ShipBob Mid-Market quoted at $5.10 pick+pack + $35/pallet storage + $40/hr receiving → STRONG 3PL with $4.15/order advantage). State persists to localStorage (`ecom-ops:inventory-cost-comparator:v1`). Copy-report emits a paste-ready markdown handoff. **Cross-page intelligence**: on first mount, the panel reads `ecom-ops:your-store:v1` and pre-fills `monthlyOrders` + `aov` from the operator's Your-store card on Overview; re-hydrates on focus if Overview inputs are updated. Sidebar nav entry was already present at `/inventory`.
- **Why it matters:** `/inventory` was the operator surface with the most gutted interactive coverage — 4 static research tables and 1 hand-curated CCC block, but no way to translate "MY in-house cost + MY 3PL quote" into a unit-economics verdict. The page already linked `Move #12 / research/05` and was adjacent to `/3pl` (which has a path-A/B/C selector scorer), but the gap was the *cost-only comparator* — the thing an operator actually needs to decide "stay in-house or migrate to 3PL". This calculator closes that gap without duplicating the path-selector on `/3pl`. The Your-store overlay means an operator who hasn't tuned their inputs sees numbers generated from their real monthlyOrders + aov, not industry medians.
- **Files added:** `dashboard/src/lib/inventory-cost-comparator.ts` (552 lines, pure rule engine — `computeInventoryCost()` + `validateInventoryInputs()` + `inventoryVerdictTag()` + `renderInventoryCostMarkdown()` + `mergeFromYourStoreForInventory()` + 5-bucket verdict thresholds), `dashboard/src/components/inventory-cost-comparator.tsx` (685 lines, client component with 10 in-house + 4 3PL input fields + side-by-side cost-stack output + 4-metric delta bar + verdict tag + copy report), `dashboard/src/lib/__tests__/inventory-cost-comparator.test.ts` (42 pure-rule-engine assertions — defaults bench, 5 verdict buckets with hand-tuned fixture overrides, break-even math under 4 branches of fixed/variable sign combinations, storage merge + validation + markdown render).
- **Files modified:** `dashboard/app/inventory/page.tsx` (mounted `<InventoryCostComparator />` between the page hero and the static 3PL-vs-in-house card; updated hero copy to "The calculator below turns those benchmarks into YOUR monthly cost stack + a 5-bucket verdict + break-even volume").
- **Verification:** (a) RED-GREEN — feature was absent (`grep -E "Cost Comparator|PathCalculator|verdict" dashboard/app/inventory/page.tsx` → 0 matches) before this tick. ## 2026-09-26 18:37 UTC — /playbooks: per-playbook phased progress tracker (16/30 playbooks decomposed into N actionable phases)

- **What shipped:** New `<PhasedProgress />` component on `/playbooks` between the ROI calculators and the binary "Mark shipped" tracker. Parses each playbook's `## Step-by-step` H3 sub-headings into a tickable checklist at build time (16 playbooks have phases); operator checks off each step as they ship it. State persists to localStorage (`ecom-ops:playbook-phases:v1`) so per-step progress survives reloads and is per-browser. Fleet-wide strip reports "X / N phases · pct%" with copy-report (paste-ready markdown), reset-all, and per-playbook Mark-all-phases / Reset-phases / Open-playbook actions. A "Mark shipped now" affordance surfaces when all phases of a playbook are complete but the binary toggle hasn't been flipped yet (so the operator can still pin shipped-on date + note). Compact `PhasedProgressStrip` card mirrors the binary `ShippedProgressStrip` on `/`.
- **Why it matters:** The shipped-playbooks tracker is binary — operator can't see "65% through PB-04 welcome series, 5 of 9 steps live" without re-reading the playbook markdown. The phased tracker closes that gap: per-step granularity + a fleet-wide rolled-up percentage that answers "what % of the playbook library have I actually built?" The 16-of-30 ratio is honest — playbooks without a `## Step-by-step` section (research docs retrofitted as playbooks, strategy/marketing pieces) are excluded from the denominator so they don't drag the average to 0.
- **Files added:** `dashboard/src/components/phased-progress.tsx` (~440 lines, main + `FleetStrip` + `PlaybookPhasedRow` + `PhasedProgressStrip`), `dashboard/src/lib/playbook-phases.ts` (~190 lines, storage + `togglePhase`/`markAllPhases`/`clearPlaybookPhases`/`clearAllPhasedProgress` + `pctComplete` + `totalProgressPct` + `renderPhasedMarkdown` + intent bands)
- **Files modified:** `dashboard/scripts/parse-content.mjs` (new `playbook.phases` extraction — walk section list, capture H3 sections between Step-by-step H2 and next H2; stable id = `${order}-${slug}`), `dashboard/src/lib/content.ts` (`PlaybookPhase` interface + `Playbook.phases?`), `dashboard/src/lib/content.json` (re-parsed: 30 playbooks, 16 with phases; PB-01 9 phases, PB-02 7 phases, PB-03 24 phases, etc.), `dashboard/app/playbooks/page.tsx` (mount `<PhasedProgress />` above `<ShippedPlaybooksTracker>`), `dashboard/app/page.tsx` (mount `<PhasedProgressStrip />` next to `<ShippedProgressStrip>` on `/`)
- **Verification:** RED-GREEN — feature absent pre-tick. GREEN — `node scripts/parse-content.mjs` re-parsed and emitted `wrote /data/workspace/ecommerce-ops/dashboard/src/lib/content.json — 30 playbooks` (16 with phases). `cd dashboard && NEXT_TELEMETRY_DISABLED=1 npm run build` SUCCEEDED on first attempt with zero errors and zero warnings; /playbooks bundle emitted at 2.84 kB / 868 kB First Load JS. `vercel deploy --prod --yes` SUCCEEDED — hostname `dashboard-fsjhhl64z-mattiasadem-5021s-projects.vercel.app`, READY/target=production. `vercel alias set … ecommerce-ops-iota.vercel.app` SUCCEEDED in 1s. Move #358.2 tells the operator when the primary warehouse is at-capacity and how to overflow-route. Move #357 tells the operator how many units to buy. **None of them tell the operator: "should we bypass the warehouse entirely and have the supplier ship direct to the customer when supplier-lead-time < transfer-lead-time?".** Move #358.3 fills that gap with a 6-pillar per-SKU-supplier-drop-ship framework + per-SKU-supplier-lead-time-vector 5-dim + per-SKU-transfer-lead-time-vector 4-dim + per-SKU-drop-ship-eligibility-decision-engine 7-condition gate + per-supplier-drop-ship-throughput-vector 4-dim + per-SKU-drop-ship-cost-vs-transfer-cost calculator 4-term math + AUTO-EXECUTE-DROP-SHIP / OPERATOR-APPROVAL / OPERATOR-ESCALATE / REJECT routing + per-SKU-drop-ship-rollback-decision-engine 4-sub-rule R1-R4 + per-supplier risk-tier-scoring-engine + Triple-Whale per-drop-ship-event-stream 22-field schema. The 18 pitfalls (P1-P18) cover the most common operator mistakes including P1 (ship-without-per-SKU-supplier-lead-time-vector → 30d-baseline-missing), P3 (no-supplier-p90-vs-transfer-p90-threshold → 5× stockout at BFCM-peak), P4 (no-supplier-BFCM-peak-capacity-overlay → Q4 throttle), P7 (no-confidence-band-on-decision → operators distrust auto-decisions), P9-R1 (supplier-p90-spike rollback missing → stockouts surge at day 30), P13 (no-Move-#358.2-CLI-band-overlay → AT-CAPACITY-and-CRITICAL escalation missed). Year-1 ROI band 6:1-18:1 with default 9:1 at $3M GMV Path B (drop-ship-cost-savings 12-25% × 30%-drop-ship-eligible-SKUs + BFCM-peak-stockout-avoidance 8-15% × $200k-BFCM-stockout-exposure + per-SKU-margin-preservation 2-5pp × 100%-catalog + transfer-cost-amortization-acceleration 15-30% × 70%-transfer-routed-SKUs + holding-cost-reduction 20-40% × 30%-drop-ship-routed-SKUs + supplier-cost-amortization-tracking via Triple-Whale per-drop-ship-event-stream = aggregate-lift 5-12% of total-COGS-or-$150k-$360k at $3M-GMV baseline / $20k-$50k operator-cost = 6:1-18:1 ratio with default 9:1).

Move #9 (PDP) tells the operator how to design a single PDP. Move #10 (A/B testing) tells the operator how to A/B test PDP-hypotheses. Move #13 (promo-calendar) tells the operator when to discount. Move #95 (payments) tells the operator how to route payments. **None of them tell the operator which SKUs go WHERE.** Move #356 fills that gap with a 6-pillar framework + 5-dimension per-SKU scoring rubric (D1 90-day-revenue 25% + D2 repeat-purchase-rate 20% + D3 reviews 15% + D4 margin×velocity 25% + D5 hero-creative-fit 15%) + 3-tier catalog hierarchy (hero 8-15% / halo 25-35% / filler 50-65% of catalog) + 25-cohort per-cohort-affinity matrix (5 dimensions × 5 buckets) + 11-channel per-channel-role-mapping (homepage / collection / search / email / SMS / paid-ads / loyalty-reward / international / etc.) + cannibalization-detection + dead-stock-flagging + reorder-vs-discontinue-decision-engine + weekly-merch-meeting-cadence-engine + monthly-board-pack + quarterly-catalog-tier-rebalance. The 18 pitfalls (P1-P18) cover the most common operator mistakes including P6 (no-per-cohort-affinity-matrix → 200-cohort-affinity-leaks), P8 (homepage-features-wrong-SKUs → CVR drops 8-18%), P9 (welcome-flow-#3-features-bestseller-not-first-time-buyer-fit → CVR drops 30-45%), P10 (paid-ads-features-newest-SKU-with-zero-reviews → CAC doubles). Year-1 ROI band 6:1-18:1 with default 10:1 at $3M GMV Path B (hompage-CVR-lift 8-18% × 25%-budget-share + email-flow-RPR-lift 15-30% × 10%-budget-share + paid-social-CAC-drop 12-25% × 10%-budget-share + LTV-weighted-mix-shift-lifts-gross-margin-2-5pp × 100%-catalog + post-purchase-upsell-attach-rate-lift-20-40% × 10%-budget-share + international-region-SKU-mix-uplift 5-12% × 5%-budget-share = aggregate-lift 8-18% of total-revenue = $240k-$540k at $3M-GMV baseline / $30k-$50k operator-cost = 6:1-18:1 ratio with default 10:1).

## 2026-09-26 13:25 UTC — /pdp-ab-launch-plan: 30-day always-on PDP A/B launch plan generator (Move #9.5)

- **What shipped:** New route at `/pdp-ab-launch-plan` with a one-click "Generate 30-day launch plan" button. Opens a modal with a 4-week day-by-day checklist (Days 1–30 across W1–W4) generated from the operator's saved PDP A/B calculator inputs (`ecom-ops:playbooks:pdp-ab:v1`) overlaid with their Your-store AOV/orders/margin (`ecom-ops:your-store:v1`). Snapshot block (verdict + annualized ratio + AOV + margin + sessions + tests/month) computed by `forecastPdpAbTest()` — same math as `scripts/pdp_ab_test.py`. Result is paste-ready markdown: copy-to-clipboard OR download `.md` (named `pdp-ab-launch-plan-YYYY-MM-DD.md`). 30 days, 4 weeks, 30 checkbox-able actions covering: tool selection, analytics instrumentation, baseline metrics, hypothesis backlog, variant build, QA across breakpoints, monitoring config, launch, interim read, final readout, Test #2 kickoff, program readout. Sidebar nav entry added (icon ▦).
- **Why it matters:** Closes the gap between "you've tweaked the calculator inputs" and "the 30-day program is actually in your project tracker". The PDP A/B playbook already lists the days in markdown; this turns the playbook into a one-click artifact that drops straight into Linear / Notion / Google Cal / Slack without the operator having to re-type. The Your-store overlay means a DTC operator who hasn't opened the playbook sees the plan generated from their real AOV / margin / monthly orders, not industry medians.
- **Where to see it:** https://ecommerce-ops-iota.vercel.app/pdp-ab-launch-plan
- **Feature class:** one-click-action + state-persistence + cross-page-intelligence (reads PDP A/B inputs from /playbooks + Your-store from /)

## 2026-09-27 14:50 UTC — /welcome-series-launch-plan: 30-day always-on Welcome Series launch plan generator (Move #3.4)

- **What shipped:** New route at `/welcome-series-launch-plan` with a one-click "Generate 30-day launch plan" button. Opens a modal with a 30-day day-by-day checklist (Days 1–30 across W1–W4) generated from the operator's saved Welcome Series ROI calculator inputs (`ecom-ops:playbooks:ws-roi:v1`) overlaid with their Your-store AOV/orders/margin (`ecom-ops:your-store:v1`). Snapshot block (verdict + ROI ratio + net/month + breakeven CVR + opt-ins/AOV/margin/discount/send-cost) computed by `forecastWelcomeSeries()` — same math as `scripts/welcome_series_roi.py`. Result is paste-ready markdown: copy-to-clipboard OR download `.md` (named `welcome-series-launch-plan-YYYY-MM-DD.md`). 30 days, 4 weeks, 30 checkbox-able actions covering: opt-in source audit, welcome-discount decision, Klaviyo + Postscript flow scaffolds, 5 emails (welcome/social-proof/education/founder/last-chance), SMS opt-in wiring, Triple Whale mapping, soft-launch to 10%, ramp to 100%, 14-day CVR readout, per-email engagement deep-dive, unsub cohort analysis, SMS attribution readout, paid-acquisition segment split, next-month A/B queue, 30-day program readout. CTA button also surfaced on the Welcome Series ROI calculator on `/playbooks` (next to "Reset defaults" and "Copy report") for at-handbook discovery. Sidebar nav entry added (icon ▦).
- **Why it matters:** Companion to the PDP A/B launch plan shipped on the prior tick — closes the same gap for the second-largest retention-lever flow. Operators typically have the 5-email + 3-SMS welcome-series template ready in Klaviyo but no calendar-paced checklist to drive the launch. The plan reads the operator's REAL AOV/margin/opt-ins so a $500k DTC operator who hasn't tweaked the calc gets the plan generated from their real numbers, not $1k/3%/$75/70% defaults. Cross-page intelligence: reads from both `ws-roi:v1` and `your-store:v1` so the snapshot is consistent across Overview, Playbooks, and the launch-plan route.
- **Where to see it:** https://ecommerce-ops-iota.vercel.app/welcome-series-launch-plan — or click "📅 30-day launch plan" on the Welcome Series ROI calculator on https://ecommerce-ops-iota.vercel.app/playbooks

## 2026-09-27 18:10 UTC — /inventory Stockout-risk calculator (interactive-tool + state-persistence + cross-page-intelligence)

- **What shipped:** New interactive calculator mounted on `/inventory` between the 3PL-vs-In-house Cost Comparator and the reference tables. The /inventory page historically surfaced only static research tables (3PL vs in-house cost stack + Major 3PLs pricing + forecasting basics + CCC); the calculator turns it into the operator's actual weekly-stockout dashboard. 8 inputs: monthlyOrders + AOV + gross margin (auto-filled from `ecom-ops:your-store:v1` on Overview — cross-page intelligence) + onHandUnits + weeklySellThroughPct + supplierLeadTimeDays + safetyStockWeeks + reorderCadenceDays + wholesaleCostPctOfAov. Outputs: weeks-of-stock + expected-stockout-in-days + reorder-arrival-window + days-of-cover-gap (positive = stockout risk) + lost-orders-if-stockout + lost-revenue-if-stockout + lost-margin-if-stockout + recommended PO size + wholesale PO cost + annual-carry-cost estimate + a 5-band verdict (STOCKOUT IMMINENT / TIGHT — REORDER THIS WEEK / WATCH / HEALTHY / OVERSTOCKED — CAPITAL DRAG). Reorder-now flag fires when reorder arrival window > expected stockout date. Copy-audit-report button emits a paste-ready markdown report (standup / Notion / Slack). Persistence: `ecom-ops:stockout-risk:v1` (full inputs map); "Save as my numbers" button opts into persistence (defaults don't auto-save so the operator who just landed from the nav doesn't get their visitor-state committed before they intend).
- **Why it matters:** Closes the second of two gaps on `/inventory` (3PL cost comparator already shipped; stockout math wasn't). The canonical DTC inventory rule — "if reorder arrival > cover, you're losing orders" — is currently buried in a research table with no operator-facing calculator. This calculator turns the rule into a per-operator verdict: at the canonical $1M-GMV apparel defaults (1,000 orders/mo, $75 AOV, 2,000 on-hand, 25% sell-through/wk, 21d lead, 14d cadence, 2wk buffer) the math fires "STOCKOUT IMMINENT" (4-week cover ≤ 28d arrival window). Cross-tab + same-tab sync means updating Your-store on Overview while viewing /inventory triggers a re-hydration without a remount.
- **Where to see it:** https://ecommerce-ops-iota.vercel.app/inventory — the stockout-risk card sits right below the 3PL cost comparator; AOV/orders/margin fields auto-fill from Your-store on first mount. Or click "Save as my numbers" to persist a specific scenario (e.g. entering your real lead time + sell-through for SKB-12 hoodie).
- **Feature class:** interactive-tool + state-persistence + cross-page-intelligence + one-click-action (copy audit)

Defaults match the Python CLI's $5M US DTC + $10M Amazon Path B baseline (3.5:1-35:1 Year-1 ROI).
